“My fame is not that low.”

“Right. I see. Then it is good enough.”

Dan Ja-Kang smiled brightly.

“Your death will definitely be known to the world. So much so that everyone will know that we are here.”

Desolate Gold Elder’s eyes, which had been calm the entire time, twitched for a moment.

“What you want...”

“Now shut your filthy mouth, you disgusting infidel. Just talking to you is rotting my ears.”

Blood began to pour from Dan Ja-Kang’s eyes.

“All I need is your last words. Shout, shout until your throat bursts, so that your insignificant screams will echo throughout the world.”

“Fucking...”

The expression on Desolate Gold Elder’s face changed.

The madness emanating from Dan Ja-Kang was suffocating him.

“Great lord!”

“Negotiations are broken off. We will start by striking the head of the enemy!”

The good news, however, was that they were right in front of the enemy leader. If they could just eliminate that one man, the followers of the enemy would be in shambles!

“Kill them!”

As soon as Desolate Gold Elder’s order was given, the elite of the Black Ghost Tower charged towards the High Priest, emitting killing intent.

But at that moment.

Kwaaaang!

A storm of unimaginable demonic qi swept through Dan Ja-Kang. The black qi surged endlessly into the sky like the giant fist of a dragon.

In front of that magnificent sight, the elite of the Black Ghost Tower who were rushing in stopped in their tracks.

“Uh... hh...”

Is this really a sight created by humans?

Blood light poured out from the ominously dark and black vortex.

“Only death will make you worthy!”

Demonic qi from Dan Ja-Kang gushed out in all directions.

Chapter 1023: What Did You Just Say? (8)

Humans forget.

Humans who have swallowed the poison of time slowly forget everything: what they learned, what they heard, even what they experienced.

That is why humans can challenge again.

Even those who have experienced heartbreaking failure can challenge again because of forgetting, and those who have suffered excruciating pain can move ahead again because they forget.

Perhaps the majority of the dazzling progress humans have made has been a gift brought about by this ‘forgetting’.

However, at this moment, everyone in Black Ghost Tower painfully realized:

There are things in the world that must never be forgotten. Things that must be remembered no matter the price to pay... which they had now forgotten.

Kwaaaak!

This incomparable black vortex seemed to deeply, deeply suck in the hearts of those who witnessed it with their own eyes.

“Uh... uh...”

They were out of breath. A thick, oppressive aura pressed down on them. It was as if they were submerged in the deep sea.

Even the elite of Black Ghost Tower, even Desolate Gold Elder, known as the jewels of the Black Ghost Tower and the best swordsmen in the Evil Faction, were speechless at the incredible sight before them.

‘This... is Demonic Arts?’

First, his body began to tremble as he felt this strange sense of crisis.

It wasn’t simply because the man was strong. This was something different. The sight unfolding before his eyes now seemed to deny all the paths of martial arts he had known until then.

Justice, Evil, and even the Outside the Wall sects—all martial arts held an essence of their own.

What should they call that Martial Arts? No, that strange thing isn’t even worthy of being called Martial Arts.

Kuaaaak!

The sound of the swirling demonic qi tearing through the air sounded like someone’s desperate scream.

Black smoke billowed out and swirled around like a storm, enveloping the High Priest on all sides. The wind swept up, uprooting trees and turning the ground upside down.

Before they could resist the qi, they had to crouch down to avoid being blown far away by the wind.

But at that moment, something strange happened that made them doubt their eyes.

Among those who were at a loss, the two people in the lead began to be gradually sucked forward.

“W-what!”

The warriors were shocked at the gravitational force they felt from ahead of them.

The qi swirling like a storm was clearly pushing them away. However, their bodies were being pulled forward and forward, ridiculously. The fate of those who hesitated for a moment, unable to respond to the absurd situation, was extremely harsh.

“U-uh?”

The two in the lead, who had been forcibly moved ahead, were eventually sucked in at this incredible speed, like they had thrown themselves at the enemy with all their might.

Until then, they hadn’t even figured out what was happening, but then, they saw it. A vortex of black demonic qi was spinning fiercely, welcoming them.

It was like a dark mouth.

“Eu-eukkkkk!”

A desperate scream burst out from the mouths of those who sensed their fate. The storm of black demonic qi soon devoured their bodies.

Kwakwakwakk!

Cut. Tear. Slash.

No words fit. If they had to express it in crude language, the word ‘torn apart’ would be the most appropriate one.

The human body was too weak to withstand that tremendous demonic qi. The bodies swept by the storm instantly turned into a handful of flesh. The crimson blood was caught in the vortex of black demonic qi and soared up endlessly. It seemed like the entire sky had been dyed red.

Thud.

The scattered blood splattered on the ground. It literally rained blood.

Those who watched the entire scene with their eyes wide open were unable to even breathe and just took the blood falling from the sky with their entire bodies.

‘What... is this?’

This couldn’t be understood at all.

They couldn’t hurriedly gather together and do their best, but even so, these people gathered there were the best of Black Ghost Tower, the ones brought up with care. They were the elite of the elite, comparable to any sect in the world.

They turned into a handful of blood in an instant without even being able to resist. It was a sight they couldn’t understand or accept with their common sense.

However, there was no way they wouldn’t believe it. The heat of the blood flowing down their faces made it impossible for anyone here to escape from reality.

“S-stop...!”

Swishhhh!

At that moment, three black blades shot out from the black storm and sliced through the body of the person who was about to shout.

“....”

Not even feeling the sensation of his body being cut, he lowered his head with a blank expression and looked down at his body.

Crack.

Long red lines began to be drawn vertically on his body.

“That....”

Crack!

His body split into four pieces and collapsed to the ground.

Thud.

This deep, dull noise rang out repeatedly. It was the sound of a body that had been cut down hitting the ground. The eeriness of that sound was incomparable to anything they had ever experienced in their lives.

“Uh, uh-uh....”

At that moment, another person floated into the air and was sucked into the black demonic qi storm.

“Uh, Uhhhhhhh!”

A desperate scream echoed. The one who saw the storm of demonic qi fiercely approaching his nose screamed as if he would puke out blood from his mouth at any moment.

But at that very moment, the storm of demonic qi, which seemed like it would swallow his body at any moment, disappeared as if it had been washed away.

Although his life was saved for now, he couldn’t be happy about it. A man was standing in the place where the demonic qi that had been blocking his vision like a black curtain had disappeared.

Crackle!

He grabbed the face of the man that Dan Ja-Kang had sucked in with his hand.

Woong. Woong. Clench!

Then, he slowly began to tighten his grip. The fingers, which had penetrated the man’s face, made a clear set of five holes.

“Kua... euk. Ugh....”

The one caught by Dan Ja-Kang desperately clawed and grabbed the hand holding his face.

“Ku...ruk. Gurgle....”

He scratched Dan Ja-Kang’s arm, groaning in fear that his head might explode at any moment.

The sight of a warrior, trained in martial arts and an elite of the Black Ghost Tower, desperately clawing at his arm made the onlookers feel even more despair.

“...to these insignificant things.”

Dan Ja-Kang, with his face contorted, applied more strength to the hand holding the man’s head.

Burst!

The head finally shattered into pieces, exploding all around. The body, now headless, fell helplessly to the ground like a rotten bundle of straw.

Every time the heart, which hadn’t yet stopped, beat, red blood gushed from the corpse’s neck. The blood dyed Dan Ja-Kang’s bloody robe even redder.

Crack!

Dan Ja-Kang, who had trampled the convulsing corpse, slowly approached Black Ghost Tower.

The face of Desolate Gold Elder, who had watched everything unfold, lost all color.

His heart, which had always stayed calm, lost control at that moment and began to make dissonant sounds.

‘What is he? A monster?’

The warriors with him were the elite of Black Ghost Tower, raised with great care. They were the elite of the elite, created by investing a great deal of time and money. Yet, this monster was trampling them as if crushing bugs with his fingers.

‘How can someone like that...’

Everything was exaggerated.

People always add a little bit of falsehood to what they see. So, stories passed down through word of mouth are often distorted and changed by added falsehood, eventually becoming completely different from the original.

He thought that the story about the Demonic Cult, which had dyed the Central Plains in blood a hundred years ago, couldn’t escape that rule.

If there were a hundred elite warriors of the Black Ghost Tower along with Desolate Gold Elder, they could deal with a small number of enemies at any time.

Even if the worst happened, if they could take some damage, they would be able to escape and plan for the future.

But he faced the enemy right now.

That legend, the terrible smell of blood flowing from the past, had now faded to the point of being nothing. That smell of death was so vivid that it couldn’t be denied.

There was not even an exaggeration in the story about the Demonic Cult.

Thud. Thud.

As Dan Ja-Kang gradually approached, the elite of Black Ghost Forest shouted involuntarily.

“S-Stop him!”

“There is just one opponent!”

“Surround him and fight!”

Desolate Gold Elder was frozen in place like a stone statue.

He was the one who understood everything in the world and calculated everything too. But the ghost of the past, which was now manifesting before his eyes, was impossible to define. He didn’t know what to do.

Even in the midst of all this, those who didn’t even have the skill to estimate the size of their opponent began to move according to their instincts. Like a locust raising both forelegs towards a fiercely running cart.

Compared to the demonic qi vortex, the current high priest looked no different from an ordinary warrior, so he rushed at them, drawing out their qi.

One could call it courage. However, courage that goes beyond one’s limits is foolishness.

The high priest slowly extended one hand towards the warriors who were rushing at him while screaming.

Kuaaa!

With a cracking sound, the Black demonic qi blade, which shot out from his fingertips, tore the limbs of those rushing at him into a hundred pieces.

Swish!

Flesh and blood poured down like rain.

Strangely, he didn’t feel afraid. It was more a sense of unfamiliarity than fear. The death the high priest created was so different from the death they normally witnessed.

Step. Step.

Death dyed the land a vivid red. The high priest walked on the land without care.

And at that moment, the frozen mouth of the great lord opened.

“You...”

All the sights he had seen so far had led him to one conclusion.

“You... are the Heavenly Demon of this time?”

Step.

The moment those words came, Dan Ja-Kang’s feet, which seemed like they would never stop, came to a halt. His face, which had been calm all this time, contorted for the first time.

“... What did you say?”

“I asked if you... were the Heavenly Demon.”

“Sigh....”

A hollow laugh escaped Dan Ja-Kang’s mouth.

“Hahaha.”

“....”

“Hahahahahahahaha!”

His laughter grew louder and louder until it reached the level of madness.

Everyone stared blankly at him with tired eyes.

Dan Ja-Kang, who laughed so hard that he even shed tears, soon coughed dryly, then glared at Desolate Gold Elder with a completely different face.

“How dare... a savage, who is worse than a bug, speak that glorious name with his filthy mouth?”

“....”

“You...”

Crack.

Dan Ja-Kang gritted his teeth and shouted with a face that said the devil had descended.

“You will die in the most excruciating pain in the world!”

Then, he exploded with vicious momentum and rushed straight at Desolate Gold Elder.

Chapter 1024: What Did You Just Say? (9)

“S-stop!”

The moment Dan Ja-Kang began to charge, screams erupted from the mouths of the Black Ghost Tower’s elite warriors.

Desolate Gold Elder was someone they had to protect at any cost. The thoughts, which had been close to brainwashing for many years, immediately compelled them to block the path to Desolate Gold Elder and scream to protect him.

However, their feet didn’t move as easily as their mouths.

Bloodshot eyes were gushing out.

Black demonic qi swirled around his entire body.

It was as if the vengeful spirits of the dead that Dan Ja-Kang had killed were wandering around him. That horrifying, almost destructive sight held them in place.

Their reasoning to fight and their instincts to survive clashed repeatedly.

At that moment, the Black Ghost Tower’s elite proved their worth. Those who had overcome their instinctive fear flew in and blocked the path to Desolate Gold Elder.

But the right choice doesn’t always lead to the right results.

KWANGG!

Dan Ja-Kang’s swung hand hit the head of the person blocking his way.

KWAANGG!

With a tremendous explosion, the shattered skulls shot out in all directions like assassin weapons.

The small bone fragments were only the size of a fingertip, but the power contained in them shattered the raised self-defense weapon like a piece of paper. And that wasn’t all—the bone fragments swirled and pierced the weak human bodies.

“Kuaaakkkk!”

The hole in his chest was only the size of a fingernail at best, but the wound on the back was big enough to fit a person’s hand in.

Dan Ja-Kang, who had instantly turned a dozen or so people who had rushed at him into a mess, tore apart their groans with his two eyes, emitting a ferocious aura.

Blood splashed out, and flesh was torn apart.

“D-Die! You monsterrr!”

Perhaps that wasn’t courage, but recklessness, or perhaps just a tantrum.

Dan Ja-Kang thoroughly punished those who had committed presumptuous acts.

Swisssh!

The black demonic qi blades that shot out from both his hands literally sliced through the people who rushed at him. Those who had become mere flesh scattered, and a thick blood mist rose around them.

Demon Lord’s Path. The demon walks the world.

The demonic qi that emanated from him tore through the air, enveloping the surroundings and spinning around. Even the Black Ghost Tower, which had terrified the Southern island, was helpless in the face of that overwhelming force.

At that moment, Desolate Gold Elder’s foot hit the ground—not ahead, but behind.

“Block him!”

A loud roar burst from Desolate Gold Elder’s mouth.

There was just one worst-case scenario: he dies right there.

Given the nature of the Evil Faction, a lord falling meant annihilation. If he died, the Black Ghost Tower would collapse instantly, so that was something he had to avoid.

Desolate Gold Elder, having finished calculating, pulled himself out with all his might.

No matter how dire the situation, he had to return alive. That way, he could tell the world about the existence of that insane monster.

But Dan Ja-Kang wasn’t merciful enough to let his prey escape on his own.

Paaaat!

Those blocking Desolate Gold Elder were scattered in disarray. Dan Ja-Kang, who had crushed, trampled, and pierced everything in front of him, rushed straight towards Desolate Gold Elder like a black arrow.

“Kuak!”

A ray of light shot out from Desolate Gold Elder’s waist.

Ghost King’s Incarnation!

His pride and joy in the world cut through the air—a sword slash that was close to perfection, without any waste. How many people had disappeared into a handful of souls due to this lightning-swift sword strike?

But.

Kakakang!

The sword slash that Desolate Gold Elder unleashed collided with Dan Ja-Kang’s hand and bounced back. Desolate Gold Elder opened his eyes wide in shock.

‘He clashed my sword with his hand?’

Kyaaak!

At that moment, a massive demonic qi blade shot out from the tip of Dan Ja-Kang’s raised hand. The demonic qi blade, resembling the claws of Asura descending upon this world, rained down on Desolate Gold Elder’s head.

Desolate Gold Elder, not daring to block it, kicked the ground and rolled to the side to avoid it.

Kwaang!

A huge scar was engraved on the ground where the demonic qi blade had fallen, as if scratched by a monster’s claws. It was a sight hard to believe even after witnessing it firsthand.

“You dirty unbeliever!”

Dan Ja-Kang threw his head back and roared like an animal.

Desolate Gold Elder’s entire body began to soak in cold sweat.

Why was this happening?

The one overwhelming this place was, of course, Dan Ja-Kang. Black Ghost Tower couldn’t even stop him. So why was that man running wild like a wounded beast?

“This!”

Dan Ja-Kang’s hands began to turn black.

“I will not kill you easily!”

The life in his eyes boiled like lava. Dan Ja-Kang shouted as if he couldn’t control his anger. The veins and tendons on his face began to bulge.

“I will tear apart the mouth that dares to hold Him down! You unbeliever!”

Kwaang!

Dan Ja-Kang, who had stomped hard on the ground, rushed once again at Desolate Gold Elder with incredible speed.

Desolate Gold Elder quickly dodged by throwing his body to the side.

Kwaang!

The ground where Dan Ja-Kang’s fist landed crumbled like tofu, exploding and shooting up as if a shell had landed in water.

Paaat!

Desolate Gold Elder, who dodged the attack by flying his body away, instantly turned around and unleashed his swift sword towards Dan Ja-Kang.

The three rays of sword light, sharp enough to be described as nothing short of razor-sharp, flew in at a speed like light.

A normal person would dodge, and someone who wasn’t would block. However, Dan Ja-Kang neither dodges nor blocks. Instead, he wraps his entire body in demonic qi and charges towards the sword qi.

‘What?’

Kwaang!

The black demonic qi that burst out deflects the three sword qis flying towards him. Desolate Gold Elder reflexively kicks the ground and flies back.

One cannot avoid it perfectly forever.

Crack!

Dan Ja-Kang’s hand, swung like a beast’s paw, sweeps across Desolate Gold Elder’s chest. A handful of flesh is torn from his body, and blood rushes out from his chest.

“Where are you trying to run! You little rat!”

Dan Ja-Kang’s appearance chasing Desolate Gold Elder is like a hawk swooping down on its prey. Desolate Gold Elder, concerned, clenches his teeth and grabs his sword.

His technique, the 12th form of the Ghost King’s Incarnation, the Ghost King’s Calamity, erupts more intensely than at any moment in his life.

Kwak! Kwaak!

The demonic qi surrounding Dan Ja-Kang and his sword qi clash fiercely in the air.

“Euhhhh.”

With a roar that is hard to believe came from Desolate Gold Elder’s mouth, the black sword rips through the demonic qi of Dan Ja-Kang.

Pat! Paat!

In an instant, crimson blood lines are drawn all over Dan Ja-Kang.

“YOUUUU!”

The sword of the Ghost King’s Calamity, which rushes out one after another, pierces through the gaps in the torn demonic qi at lightning speed.

Crack!

“....”

Desolate Gold Elder’s eyes shake for a moment.

The tip of the sword, covered in dazzling blue-black sword qi, is stuck in the center of Dan Ja-Kang’s chest.

‘Uh...’

It is an unbelievable achievement, even though he accomplished it himself.

That is why Desolate Gold Elder is momentarily gripped by fear.

‘Could it be some trap...’

Crack!

At that moment, Dan Ja-Kang grabs the sword stuck in his chest with his bare hands.

“... You fucking rat.”

Desolate Gold Elder instinctively dropped his sword and jumped back. However, before he could even escape, Dan Ja-Kang grabbed his arm.

“Euhhhhh!”

At that moment, a scream burst from Desolate Gold Elder’s mouth. Dan Ja-Kang’s nails pierced his flesh and embedded themselves in his bones. An intense burning sensation spread, as if his entire arm was on fire. Desolate Gold Elder’s body began to tremble as if he had been struck by a chill.

Yet, what filled Desolate Gold Elder with more despair than the immense pain was the sight before him. The sword that had pierced Dan Ja-Kang’s heart was being pushed out on its own.

“Kua... Eukkk...”

His bloodshot eyes widened in agony. In an instant, red flesh filled Dan Ja-Kang’s chest where a gaping wound had been.

Dan Ja-Kang spoke in a dark voice.

“I will keep you alive. You will not be granted a comfortable death. But... this body is useless.”

Suddenly, a pain so intense that his mouth fell open engulfed Desolate Gold Elder’s arm. The arm, once filled with muscle, began to wither and twist like an ancient, dying tree.

“Kuakkkk!”

A desperate scream, beyond pitiful, burst from Desolate Gold Elder’s gaping mouth.

At that moment, members of the Black Ghost Tower, who had barely caught up with the two men, screamed and rushed toward Dan Ja-Kang.

“Protect the lord!”

“Die! You monsterrrr!”

With bloodshot eyes, they rushed toward Dan Ja-Kang’s back, disregarding their own lives.

Dan Ja-Kang didn’t even turn his head. He simply swung his arms at those charging at him. The black demonic qi blades that rose up tore through all who approached.

Humans turned to flesh, and their weapons turned to dust and bounced off.

But the members of the Black Tower Ghost rushed at him without caring about their own lives. The reason was simple: the moment Desolate Gold Elder died, the entire sect would perish too. They had no other choice.

Kakang!

A warrior swung his sword even as his lower body was severed. It moved to stab Dan Ja-Kang in the shoulder, while another, whose body was crushed, grabbed Dan Ja-Kang’s leg with his remaining arm and held on.

“This!”

In the end, Dan Ja-Kang turned around, blood gushing from his eyes.

“You disgusting unbelievers!”

At the same time, dozens of demonic qi blades shot out simultaneously.

“Die, you all!”

Kwaaak!

A demonic qi blade, larger than a human body, spun fiercely and struck those rushing in. Flesh and blood mixed with his sword, creating a sight that should never exist in the human world.

Kwaaaak!

A storm of demonic qi swept forward and swept again.

And in an instant, everything vanished as if it were an illusion.

The world was filled with silence.

Only the red-tinted ground proved that everything that had happened there was not a lie.

Dan Ja-Kang, still looking around with a face that hadn’t yet calmed from his excitement, slowly glanced down at what he was holding in his hand.

Someone’s arm, twisted like an old tree, had been severed from the body’s shoulder. A faint smile formed on Dan Ja-Kang’s lips. He mumbled, throwing the arm to the ground.

“He sure is a quick thinker.”

Taking advantage of the brief moment when Dan Ja-Kang lost his senses in the fight, Desolate Gold Elder had cut off his own arm and fled.

If it were his normal self, he wouldn’t have ever given someone a chance to run away... but someone who has raised their demonic qi to the limit will lose their senses for a moment. This is the side effect he has no choice but to endure.

However, even in the murim world, a guy who was famous cut off his own arm and ran, abandoning all his subordinates.

“These people....”

Dan Ja-Kang bit his lips until they bled.

But soon, as if trying to shake off the growing suspicion, he shook his head and mumbled out of habit.

“Second Coming of Heavenly Demon.”

He will descend.

He will definitely descend.

“Second Coming of Heavenly Demon.”

In this way, he will negate all the delusions within him and finally give him a name.

Only then will Dan Ja-Kang find the meaning of existence.

“Jeok-Il.”

“Yes, High Priest.”

The man who responded to the command appeared before him and prostrated himself.

“... Kill more people, and inflict more pain on them. So that the whole world will groan in despair and pain.”

“Yes, High Priest.”

Dan Ja-Kang stared blankly at the blood-soaked land.

‘Are you listening? Heavenly Demon. Our cries.’

After a long while, he slowly turned around.

In the place where they had left, only the twisted arm of Desolate Gold Elder was left, like an old tree.

Chapter 1025: What Did You Just Say? (10)

“Hmm.”

A bitter smile spread across Jang Ilso’s lips as he held a letter in his hand.

“Demonic Cult, eh...”

Jang Ilso closed his eyes slightly, as if he needed time to organize his thoughts. Normally, Ho Ga-Myung would not have interrupted his contemplation.

However, Ho Ga-Myung was now keenly aware that Jang Ilso’s patience was wearing thin. In the end, he couldn’t hold back and spoke softly.

“Alliance lord...”

Jang Ilso opened his eyes, which were much darker than usual.

“Demonic Cult, right.”

Jang Ilso let out a hollow laugh.

“There are always variables in the world, but this was something I never expected.”

The accessories on his body jingled as he slowly raised his hand and lightly touched his face, lost in thought once again.

A deep sense of discomfort enveloped his entire being.

It felt as if all his plans were unraveling because of the one variable called the Demonic Cult. He slowly ran his tongue over his lips, and his eyes shone ominously.

“... First, we decided to respond to Black Ghost Tower...”

“It will fail.”

Ho Ga-Myung flinched at the decisive tone.

“Desolate Gold Elder believes he can calculate everything. He thinks that when the calculations fail, it is due to a lack of information and analysis. Of course, most of his thoughts are correct...”

Jang Ilso spoke with a tone laced with killing intent.

“But not this time.”

“....”

“There are definitely beings in the world that defy common sense. The Demonic Cult is one of those rare groups.”

Jang Ilso shook his head.

He didn’t know much about the Demonic Cult. However, he knew one thing for sure: there is no way to communicate with those who are devoted to fanaticism. The world they see and the world seen by those who live for themselves are entirely different.

“There is no way that those who live in different worlds can communicate with each other. Desolate Gold Elder did something foolish.”

“Then....”

“The variables that occurred were the worst, and even the initial response was unprecedented...”

Jang Ilso taunted himself, twisting the corners of his mouth.

“Such a terrible sight.”

He slowly rose from the chair.

A cold voice flowed from his mouth as he stared at the riverside for a moment, as if gathering his thoughts.

“Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, Alliance Lord.”

“Inform those across the river that the Demonic Cult has appeared.”

“... Is it alright? They will definitely try to take advantage of this situation.”

“Of course.”

Jang Ilso never believed in words like agreement and harmony. Those are just made up by the well-off people to justify their actions.

Of course, they seem to believe the words they made up themselves....

“It doesn’t matter. We’re already in a mess now. At least we should make them fall into the same mess. I cannot let them watch leisurely while wearing those clean clothes.”

“... I will do so then.”

Kakaka!

Jang Ilso’s ring rubbed roughly.

“Put away the alcohol.”

“Yes.”

“Tell everyone not to approach me for a while.”

Ho Ga-Myung nodded vigorously.

Jang Ilso silently stared at the riverside. Ho Ga-Myung, who had been watching his back for a moment, quietly but quickly retreated.

“... Demonic Cult?”

The abbot opened his mouth, seemingly dumbfounded. His eyes trembled.

As soon as the word ‘Demonic Cult’ was mentioned, the atmosphere in the room froze colder than the North Sea. To them, just mentioning ‘Demonic Cult’ was heavy and frightening.

“... Did you just say Demonic Cult?”

“Yes, Abbot! They say that the Demonic Cult has just started in Hangzhou.”

“Started?”

Cold sweat formed on the forehead of the abbot.

The start of the Demonic Cult. Even the abbot of the world couldn’t help but groan at those words.

‘What is this out of the blue?’

Of course, it isn’t completely absurd. The Demonic Cult has always been like that.

Their outbursts always happen without warning. They hold their breath as if they don’t exist, and then all of a sudden, they all stand up.

Wasn’t the war a hundred years ago the same?

The abbot, who was biting his lip, glared at Beop Gye.

“How did you get this information?”

“...The Four Sects Alliance side relayed this to me.”

The face of the abbot distorted at Beop Gye’s answer. Jong Ri-Hyung asked the question he couldn’t bring himself to ask.

“Is that information correct?”

“...What do you mean?”

“If the source of the information is from the Four Sects Alliance, then there is no way we cannot be suspicious. That Jang Ilso has been harassing us with all sorts of schemes till now. There is no evidence that this information itself isn’t Jang Ilso’s work.”

Beop Gye too nodded as if it made sense.

“I haven’t verified the information yet. However, since the information was passed down orally, I thought I should report it first before verifying it.”

At those words, Jong Ri-Hyung nodded this time. Certainly, information about the Demonic Cult wasn’t something to waste time with.

Beop Gye opened his mouth in a heavy voice.

“Beop Gye.”

“Yes!”

“Convey this situation to the Beggars Union and tell them that we must confirm the facts as quickly as possible.”

“Yes!”

“This is an urgent matter. We must find out the truth no matter the sacrifices made.”

“I will keep that in mind!”

Beop Gye ran out right away. The abbot, who was watching, couldn’t help but let out a sigh.

‘Demonic Cult...’

Why did their name come out at this moment?

“Abbot...”

“Let’s wait a moment.”

The abbot spoke as if he was taking the initiative.

“Even though the Beggars Union has not been able to properly obtain information from the Southern Lands for the past three years, if it is true that the Demonic Cult has started to move from Hangzhou, those facts can be confirmed soon. There are more than a few beggars in Hangzhou.”

“Of course.”

“It’s not too late to decide on a response after confirming the facts.”

Jong Ri-Hyung slowly nodded. His expression was trying to look calm, but his heart was pounding, and he didn’t know what to do. Since hearing the word ‘Demonic Cult,’ he couldn’t calm down no matter what he did.

“But then, abbot.”

At that moment, Peng Yeop, who had been silent behind him, spoke up.

“If by any chance the Demonic Cult’s start is true, what are you going to do then?”

The abbot looked up and then at Peng Yeop. Jong Ri-Hyung, who couldn’t bring himself to open his mouth, asked back.

“What do you mean? What is the...”

“We already experienced it painfully a hundred years ago that the Demonic Cult is not something that a single sect can handle.”

“....”

“A hundred years ago, all sects, regardless of their honor and social standing, joined forces to deal with the Demonic Cult. That means if the Demonic Cult really did start, we might have to join hands with the Four Sects Alliance.”

“... that is...”

Only then did Jong Ri-Hyung’s expression change as if he had finally grasped the situation.

Joining hands with the Four Sects Alliance.

It might be inevitable if there is an enemy like the Demonic Cult, but he still felt a strong sense of rejection. Joining hands with the Four Sects Alliance would be like turning everything they had done in the Yangtze River for nothing and throwing the justification they had claimed as a justice faction into the siege.

“If the place where the Demonic Cult originated is Hangzhou, then we must wait for them to cross that river. Do you really intend to do that, abbot?”

The abbot slowly closed his eyes.

After remaining silent for a long time, he finally opened his eyes and said.

“First let us confirm the facts and then discuss about the countermeasures.”

“Abbot, that...”

“Lord.”

The abbot looked right at Peng Yeop.

“This is not something which can be concluded based on assumptions. Words can easily lead a person to ruin, so I ask you to remain calm for now.”

“... I understand.”

A heavy silence descended on the room after the gloomy voice of Peng Yeop.

In the thick silence that made the skin tingle, the abbot quietly cried out.

However, unlike the eyes of a normal buddhist monk, his eyes looked dark and sunken.

“... What now?”

Baek Cheon and Yoon Jong opened their eyes wide.

Jo Gul, who had delivered the message quickly looked around once more. After confirming that there was no one else around to hear the conversation, he quickly whispered again.

“I heard it clearly, sasuk. Those guys from the Four Sects Alliance clearly told the Sect leader.”

“... And you heard it right?”

“This isnt something to joke about.”

Baek Cheon bit his lips.

‘Damn it, what the...’

No matter how much of a jokester Jo Gul is, he couldn’t go around using the name of Demonic Cult. If he is a disciple of Mount Hua, especially if experienced the Demonic Cult in the North Sea, even more so.

‘Demonic Cult.’

Why is the Demonic Cult suddenly popping up at this time?

His fingertips began to tremble. The Demonic Cult people he had encountered in North Sea, especially the majestic sense shown by the one called as ‘High priest’, were still vividly etched in his mind.

Just thinking about it made him feel the countless wounds he had received back then come back to him.

“What will... happen now? Sasuk?”

Even Yoon Jong, who had always maintained his composure, couldn’t hide his shock now.

“....”

How can Baek Cheon know the answer to that?

But, he was certain of one thing.

“What is that guy now?”

“That guy?”

“Chung Myung.”

Yoon Jong flinched for a moment.

What if Chung Myung heard this news?

‘No.’

Chung Myung is someone who changes at the mere mention of the word ‘Demonic Cult’. If those words reached that bastard’s ears, wasn’t it obvious what would happen? He might run off immediately with a sword in hand.

“He just went to train the Namgung family.”

Baek Cheon glared at Jo Gul.

“Have you relayed the message anywhere else?”

“Ah, no, sasuk. I came running here.”

Baek Cheon nodded with a heavy face.

“Then everyone, keep your mouth shut. Nothing is certain yet, right? It won’t be too late to relay it once everything is confirmed.”

“Yes.”

“... If he hears that the Demonic Cult has launched an attack, he might do something reckless. So first, make sure all this doesn’t reach Chung Myung’s ears... no, make sure it gets there as late as possible.”

“Yes, sas...”

It was then.

“What did you just say?”

“....”

Everyone there froze for a moment.

A voice that he absolutely did not want to hear at that very moment came from behind.

‘Ah...’

The blood drained from Baek Cheon’s face for a moment.

“Sasuk.”

Baek Cheon looked up at the sky for a moment and let out a small sigh. However, he couldn’t turn away forever. He took a deep breath and slowly turned around.

“... Chung Myung. So you see.”

Chung Myung’s expressionless face slowly changed. It was a face he wouldn’t show them unless absolutely necessary. Chung Myung exuded a deep killing intent with a wicked and evil look. It was suffocating.

That wasn’t a killing intent meant to threaten them. It was a killing intent that poured out because he couldn’t control the emotions that had burst out in an instant.

“Now...”

A rough voice flowed out from Chung Myung’s lips, as if scraping iron.

“What did you just say?”

Baek Cheon shut his eyes tightly.

Chapter 1026: But I Can Understand (1)

Baek Cheon bit his lip tightly.

‘What should I do?’

Everyone has a sore spot—a part that should never be touched, a part that reveals anger even he cannot control the moment it is touched.

For Chung Myung, that sore spot is clearly the Demonic Cult.

The problem was that Chung Myung’s reaction to his sore spot being stimulated was incomparably more intense than others. Chung Myung became a different person after hearing the words “Demonic Cult.”

Baek Cheon didn’t know why on earth Chung Myung always had to react so violently just by hearing the words “Demonic Cult.” However, what was important to Baek Cheon right now was not understanding, but responding.

“Chung Myung. This is....”

Baek Cheon tried to have a conversation, but Chung Myung’s cold voice cut him off without a trace.

“Answer me, sasuk.”

Chung Myung’s eyes were dreary.

“Who showed up?”

The overflowing killing intent was so cold. Even Baek Cheon, who knew it wasn’t directed at him, trembled for a moment.

“Chung Myung. First... calm down now.”

“Answer me first.”

“Chung Myung.”

Chung Myung’s face twisted in anger for a moment. He was almost going to explode.

At that moment, someone gently grabbed his arm.

Chung Myung turned his gaze to the person who grabbed his arm. Yu Yiseol was staring at him, as usual, with a blank expression on her face.

“Calm down.”

“....”

“Calm down.”

At that calm voice, without wavering, Chung Myung finally let out a long breath. The blood gradually returned to his lips that he had been biting.

“... okay. Now that I have calmed down, tell me.”

“....”

“Sasuk.”

Baek Cheon let out a heavy sigh.

It wouldn’t be a good idea to keep hiding it. Chung Myung would eventually find out.

Baek Cheon looked back at Jo Gul as if to explain it. Jo Gul, however, looked back and forth between Chung Myung and Baek Cheon, scratched the back of his head, and finally opened his mouth.

“Ugh. Well... I don’t know the full situation yet, but... it seems that a messenger from the Four Sects Alliance has come.”

“Four Sects Alliance?”

“That is what I heard.”

Chung Myung’s eyes darkened slightly.

“Go on.”

“... that messenger said he wanted to meet the Sect leader and came to inform that the Demonic Cult had launched their attacks on the Southern lands.”

While he was speaking, Jo Gul kept an eye on Chung Myung’s expression. But it wasn’t easy to read his emotions from the frozen, expressionless face.

“And?”

Jo Gul shook his head.

“That is all that I heard. Un Am senior sasuk took him to the Sect leader. Since they wanted the Tang family lord too, he must also be with them.”

Jo Gul mumbled uncertainly after checking Chung Myung’s expression once more.

“So, if it is something we need to know, wouldn’t they tell us right away?”

“....”

Chung Myung looked at Jo Gul and the other Five Swords in silence for a moment, then turned around and began walking. Baek Cheon shouted at him immediately.

“Yah! Where are you going!”

Chung Myung replied without even looking back.

“I have to confirm it.”

Fortunately or unfortunately, he didn’t say anything about running to the Southern Lands right away. However, the words that followed made Baek Cheon run to Chung Myung in a panic.

“From the Four Sects Alliance bastard.”

“Yah, you insane bastard!”

Baek Cheon, who ran with all his might, grabbed Chung Myung’s arm.

“Why are you going to confirm with the Four Sects Alliance, you brat!”

“....”

“Calm down for now! This isn’t something that can be done in a hurry! Right?”

“Hurry?”

Chung Myung looked back at Baek Cheon. Baek Cheon almost let go of his arm when their eyes met. Chung Myung’s eyes felt so unfamiliar to him for a moment. They were definitely Chung Myung’s eyes, but they were different from the Chung Myung he knew.

“Sasuk.”

“Yes?”

“Do you know what the Demonic Cult is?”

Baek Cheon kept his mouth shut.

Of course, he knew. He had already experienced the Demonic Cult.

But he couldn’t answer that question. Knowing a little more about the Demonic Cult than others didn’t mean he understood its essence.

“Let go.”

“... No.”

Baek Cheon bit his lip and shook his head.

“This won’t do. You’ve lost your reasoning now.”

“I get it, so let go.”

“Damn it! Why are you ignoring me...!”

Baek Cheon grabbed Chung Myung by the collar. It was right when he was about to shout.

“What are you doing!”

A loud roar pierced his ears. Baek Cheon flinched, releasing his grip on Chung Myung’s collar, and turned.

Hyun Young was glaring at them with an angry face.

“You people are doing well.”

“... Hall leader, this is....”

Baek Cheon, who had nothing to say, trailed off. Hyun Young, glaring at the two of them without hiding his anger, said coldly.

“Follow me.”

When the two didn’t move right away, he clicked his tongue and said.

“The sect leader is calling for you.”

Baek Cheon glanced back at Chung Myung. When Chung Myung, who had been silent, started walking towards Hyun Young, Baek Cheon finally let out a sigh of relief and followed him.

The Five Swords, who had been tense, also let out the breath they had been holding for so long.

Jo Gul, who wiped cold sweat from his forehead with his sleeve, looked back at Yoon Jong, unable to hide his anxious expression.

“What happens now, sahyung?”

“... well.”

Yoon Jong’s face, which was staring at Chung Myung’s back, was stiffer than usual.

“First, we should see the sect leader. We need to confirm how things are going.”

“... Yes, sahyung.”

Yoon Jong let out a short sigh as he listened to Jo Gul’s somewhat uncomfortable answer.

A heavy silence descended on the room.

Tang Gunak sat next to Hyun Jong, whose face was unusually gloomy, with the senior members of the Tang family gathered to his left and right.

It wasn’t Hyun Jong or Tang Gunak who made the atmosphere in the room so tense. It was Chung Myung sitting across from them. With his stiff face and silence, no one dared to speak up as they normally would.

It was a scene that clearly demonstrated how much influence Chung Myung had on the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

“Alliance lord.”

“... Yes.”

Perhaps because the silence had stretched too long, Tang Gunak gently urged him. Then, Hyun Jong opened his mouth in a heavy tone.

“Four Sects Alliance...”

He paused for a moment, closing his eyes tightly. Everyone sighed two or three times before he continued.

“The Four Sects Alliance has reported the situation. It seems that those presumed to be members of the Demonic Cult have appeared in Hangzhou.”

“... Demonic Cult.”

Someone groaned.

Those who were silent and those who felt helpless sighed; their feelings were not so different. The weight of the word ‘Demonic Cult’ was heavy for everyone there.

Especially...

“Demonic Cult....”

The reactions of Mount Hua’s disciples were incomparably more intense than those of the Tang family. It was only natural. Mount Hua’s feelings towards the Demonic Cult were truly complicated and dark.

“Was there nothing else to say?”

Hyun Jong shook his head at Hyun Sang’s question.

“For now, he said this is all. He mentioned he would provide additional details at another time.”

Hyun Sang nodded with a serious expression. This time, Hyun Young spoke up.

“If it was something the Four Sects Alliance asked you to convey, it must be under Jang Ilso’s instructions. Can we trust all of those words? It could be Jang Ilso’s tricks.”

But Tang Gunak shook his head.

“That is unlikely, elder.”

“Why do you think so?”

“Because the area known to have been attacked is Hangzhou.”

Tang Gunak spoke as if he was forcing his lips to part.

“The Mount Hua people will know this very well. That Demonic Cult does not discriminate between murim people and commoners. Isn’t that why they became the enemies of the public?”

Hyun Jong nodded silently.

But to be honest, he didn’t know that fact very well. The fall of Mount Hua didn’t just make people forget Mount Hua’s history. Mount Hua didn’t have much information left about the Demonic Cult either.

“Hangzhou. It is a place where numerous people live. Hangzhou, in particular, is a place where people flock from all over the world. If what the Four Sects Alliance said is true, it won’t take even a few days for the news to spread.”

“Hmm. Yes....”

“Jang Ilso wouldn’t have done something so obvious that it would be exposed.”

Hyun Young nodded, thinking that what Tang Gunak said made sense.

Besides, if what Hyun Jong said is true, the Four Sects Alliance didn’t really ask for anything. They just relayed the situation, didn’t they? No matter how much Jang Ilso’s tricks made him shiver, it was too early to suspect Jang Ilso.

“If we do that....”

At that moment, Tang Pae opened his mouth with a trembling face.

“Doesn’t that mean that the information they have passed on is likely true?”

“...”

No one responded.

“Ahem.”

Hyun Jong cleared the air with a loud cough.

“Anyway, that is the situation I have been told. So... first, I will inquire with the Beggars Union to find out what’s going on in the Southern lands, and then I will decide on the direction the Alliance will take.”

Everyone nodded. In such a situation, that was clearly the right answer.

But Hyun Jong, who said that, still had a face that was bothered by something. His gaze was fixed on the person across from him.

“Chung Myung.”

As the soft voice came, Chung Myung opened his closed eyes and stared at Hyun Jong. His gaze was eerily gloomy.

Hyun Jong let out this soft sigh and asked.

“Do you have anything else to say?”

Chung Myung remained silent for a moment.

“Chung Myung?”

“I am not sure if it would make any difference even if I spoke.”

He opened his mouth after being called out once more. His voice was sharper than usual.

“If you understood what emergence of the Demonic Cult means, you wouldn’t have said that you would just sit here and wait for information.”

“... What does that mean?”

“Heavenly Demon doesn’t care about the difference in the strong and weak. He clearly said that.”

This time, Chung Myung’s gaze turned to Tang Gunak. That was definitely what he said. Tang Gunak slowly nodded.

“... right.”

“You understand what I mean?”

The moment Tang Gunak was about ask again. Chung Myung spoke first.

“Today it was Hangzhou.”

“....”

“Tomorrow it will be Suzhou.”

Tang Gunak couldn’t bring himself to say anything.

“The next will be Wenzhou and the next Fuzhou.”

The voice was devoid of warmth. But even colder than that was the meaning behind those words.

“The village is being erased.”

Someone gulped.

“A city is being erased later.”

“....”

“Everything which has a life gets erased.”

Hyun Jong’s fingertips began to tremble at that.

“During the day that Sect leader asked us to wait.”

Chung Myung’s eyes stared at Hyun Jong like he was piercing through them.

“Not hundreds, but thousands of lives will be lost.”

“...”

“Which is why!”

Chung Myung’s voice rose for the first time.

“... There were those who fought with their lives on the line, knowing that there were those who would seek their own gain and pull their feet out from behind.”

Crack.

The sound of Chung myung’s teeth rang out in the silent room.

“Chung Myung....”

“I am not blaming you. Just understand it, sect leader.”

“....”

“What is the price of waiting for a day like that?”

Hyun Jong closed his eyes, feeling a moment of dizziness.

Chapter 1027: But I Can Understand (2)

Words are merely a medium to convey the meaning contained within them. However, sometimes the feelings embedded in the words themselves convey more than the meaning they hold.

That was what Chung Myung had just expressed.

The meaning contained within them was significant, but what was conveyed more keenly was the emotion in Chung Myung’s words.

The price that comes with just letting go and waiting for a day.

Who wouldn’t remain silent under the weight of those words?

Slowly opening his eyes, Hyun Jong met Chung Myung’s gaze. It was calm and composed, devoid of reproach or urgency.

But...

‘It is different.’

The decision might not change. However, there is a difference between making a decision knowing its cost and making one without that knowledge.

Right now, Chung Myung is telling Hyun Jong that he needs to be prepared for everything that will happen because of his one word.

That is why he was saying it.

Just understand. Just understand how much is at stake when the choice that was casually put aside is being made.

“Thousands of...”

Hyun Jong’s voice sank heavily.

“... lives.”

Those words, slowly flowing out, suffocated everyone.

How could anyone not tremble at those words? Even if there were such a person, they couldn’t be here. This is the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

“Lord.”

“Yes, Alliance leader,”

Hyun Jong looked at Tang Gunak and asked.

“What do you think about what the disciple of the sect has just said?”

“Hmm.”

Tang Gunak glanced at Chung Myung and began to speak.

“Considering the past actions of the Demonic Cult, it isn’t entirely wrong.”

“...”

“Of course, since we do not know the scale of the Demonic Cult that has just begun, the ramifications might be different... it cannot be denied that civilian casualties will increase as time goes by.”

“Then....”

“I do not know about the thousand lives...”

He could understand the implications without needing to hear all the words. It was clear that hundreds of lives were at stake.

‘So that is why.’

There were moments when he felt resentful. There were times when he secretly lamented his own foolishness.

Everyone else had withdrawn, so why did they risk their lives and stand at the forefront until the end? Why didn’t they foresee the future a little better? He once resented his ancestors for this.

But now he understood. They didn’t have the luxury of time.

They must have known what it would mean for Mount Hua to be pushed back. That’s why his ancestors killed Heavenly Demonic without worrying about the aftermath and risked everything to defeat the Demonic Cult.

Then...

If Hyun Jong’s ancestors made that choice, what decision should Hyun Jong make now, having witnessed the results of that choice?

Hyun Jong removed his hands from his face and looked at Chung Myung.

“Chung Myung.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“... I will tell you exactly what it is. I do not know what choice is the best to make right now.”

“....”

“So listen. What do you think our Mount Hua and our Heavenly Friends Alliance should do from now on?”

Chung Myung stared at him intently and spoke with gravity.

“We shouldn’t wait for the Beggars Union.”

After a pause, Chung Myung looked around at everyone and continued.

“Right now, the one who holds the information about the Southern Lands is the Four Sects Alliance. If you want to get information, then you need to hear it from the most reliable people.”

“Four Sects Alliance....”

A sigh escaped Hyun Jong’s mouth. He knew what it meant to get information from the Four Sects Alliance.

In a situation where the Nine Great Sects were already claiming that the Four Sects Alliance and the Heavenly Friends Alliance were colluding, asking them for information first would only strengthen Shaolin’s claims.

Chung Myung couldn’t have been unaware of that fact. However, he insisted that they needed to receive proper information from the Four Sects Alliance as soon as possible, even if it meant risking everything.

“As you said, the Four Sects Alliance is the first to get this information about Hangzhou, but isn’t it difficult to trust them?”

Chung Myung shook his head at Tang Gunak’s question.

“They will tell you as it is.”

“And why do you think so?”

“There is no reason to lie. If they gave us information, they must have intended to drag us into it somehow. But there’s no need to reduce the size of the enemy troops. If they exaggerated, then maybe.”

“But...”

Chung Myung stared directly at Tang Gunak.

“While you hesitate, another person might die there.”

Tang Gunak exhaled deeply.

He began to understand how absurd the situation was.

No matter how many beggars there were in Hangzhou, ordinary beggars would have limited ability to grasp the power of the Demonic Cult.

And the warriors of the Beggars Union who could properly assess the power of the Demonic Cult had all withdrawn from Hangzhou because of the treaty of Non-aggression in the Southern Land.

In the end, this meant that the only ones who could provide them with proper information about the strength of the Demonic Cult, which had emerged at this point, were the Four Sects Alliance.

‘But...’

The people holding that information are the most untrustworthy in the world, leaving Tang Gunak at a loss, unable to do anything.

Since he couldn’t trust the Four Sects Alliance, he had to verify the information. But the longer he delayed, the more the civilians would suffer.

How on earth would he resolve the situation?

“... So are you saying that we should believe the Four Sects Alliance’s words and walk towards that place called the Southern lands on our own two feet?”

“I am just speaking of the situation as it is.”

Chung Myung’s gaze shifted from Tang Gunak to Hyun Jong.

“The choice is yours.”

Those eyes weighed heavily on Hyun Jong.

“There is no such thing as a right choice, sect leader.”

Chung Myung growled.

“The world is not that clear cut. Choosing is not about finding the right answer, but rather about deciding where you want to go. Saying you will find the right choice is no different from saying you will not choose.”

Hyun Jong’s fingertips hidden in the sleeves trembled.

“But, there is no such thing as a wrong choice. They are just different choices.”

“.....”

“Sect leader, the reason the leader must exist is because there must be a moment when they must choose something that isnt divided cleanly into right and wrong. There must be someone who can decide that. no matter what you choose, it will surely be different than not deciding and putting it off.”

Hyun Jong nodded.

It was the right thing to say.

“So just choose. That is enough. The followers and disciples will follow the Alliance leader’s choice.”

Only then did Hyun Jong feel a bit better.

“Then....”

It was the very moment he was about to open his mouth to answer.

“Let me ask you one thing.”

Baek Cheon, who had been silent till then, spoke up. Hyun Jong’s eyes looking at him, flashed with a strange look.

Baek Cheon rarely spoke up first like this, not at Mount Hua’s personal meeting, but at one of Heavenly Friends Alliance. He even cut off the words of Hyun Jong.

Baek Cheon’s face, frozen cold seemed to be filled with killing intent.

“if sect leader concludes that he will not handle the Cult and will just watch from here....”

“....”

“What are you planning to do?”

That was a question directed at Chung Myung. However, Chung Myung stayed silent. Rather he didn’t even look at Baek Cheon and just continued to look at Hyun Jong, as if urging the man.

“Answer it.”

“Sect leader.”

When Chung Myung pretended not to hear and called only for Hyun Jong, Baek Cheon’s voice eventually grew louder.

“You answer, not the sect leader, you brat!”

His voice was filled with anger. It was truly rude behavior, but no one there blamed Baek Cheon for his actions.

Because everyone there heard Chung Myung’s silent answer.

This guy will go.

Even if he leaves everyone here, he will head to the place where they are, all alone. Even though the party may not know, the Mount Hua disciples clearly understood the intention of this.

Eventually, Baek Cheon grabbed the sleeve of Chung Myung and pulled him close.

“The clan and the alliance members will all follow the command of the sect leader?”

“....”

“Then you? Are you not from the sect or the alliance? Do you not need to follow? You fucking brat.”

The face of Baek Cheon, who pulled the sleeve as if it would tear, was distorted. It was an angry face that he had never shown anyone before.

“Answer!”

Chung Myung stared at Baek Cheon with indifferent eyes.

“Let go of this sasuk.”

“Sasuk? Do you think of me as your sasuk?”

“...”

“You damned.....”

“Stop it! What shameful act is this!”

In the end, Hyun Sang couldn’t stand it any longer and rebuked him. Baek Cheon bit down on his lips. Then, he reluctantly let go of the hand which was holding Chung Myung and stepped back. However, the gaze which was glaring at Chung Myung didn’t loosen up.

It wasn’t just Baek Cheon.

The other Five Swords guarding him were also glaring at Chung Myung with eyes no different from Baek Cheon.

Hyun Jong looked around at everyone with stern eyes.

In this situation, he absolutely couldn’t show any signs of wavering. Hyun Jong was a person who understood that fact more than enough.

His mouth opened, and his majestic voice flowed out.

“As the leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, and the sect leader of Mount Hua, I hereby command you.”

“Yes, Alliance leader.”

Hyun Jong’s gaze bore down on the disciples, exuding an authority befitting the leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

“Although the situation in the Southern land is urgent, it is too dangerous to enter without proper information. We need at least the bare minimum of intelligence to decide our next course of action.”

Chung Myung’s eyes darkened.

“The most reliable information will come from the Beggars Union. While discussing with them, we will also contact the nearby Nine Great Sects to explore cooperation on this issue. If we cannot obtain proper information from the Nine Great Sects, we will not hesitate to contact the Four Sects Alliance.”

“....”

“One day.”

Hyun Jong declared firmly.

“This will not last more than a day, and after that, we will decide on the direction of the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s response. Therefore, from this moment on, I strictly prohibit followers from leaving their posts. No one is to leave this mansion and must wait for further orders. This is an order from the Alliance leader. Anyone who disobeys will be severely punished!”

“I will follow the Alliance leader’s order.”

Everyone present bowed their heads towards Hyun Jong.

However, only one person, Chung Myung, didn’t bow his head and looked at Hyun Jong with cold eyes.

No one knew what was hidden in those deep eyes.

Chapter 1028: But I Can Understand (3)

His sharp, sunken eyes, his lips that were twisted and cracked, his skin which reminded him of a rough old tree, and his bloodless face.

Everything spoke of the hardships that Desolate Gold Elder had endured.

However, it wasn’t hard to guess what he had been through without even looking at his face. Above all, his arm, which had been cut off just below the shoulder bone, clearly explained the situation.

Desolate Gold Elder looked at the person sitting across from him with a haggard face. The person who was half-lying on the throne was looking down at Desolate Gold Elder with legs crossed. His gaze was extremely arrogant.

He couldn’t tell if the look in the eyes was contempt, concern, or laughter. Maybe it was everything.

Desolate Gold Elder consciously straightened his shoulders. Otherwise, his shoulders would keep hunching over on their own.

Because he committed a crime?

Of course, that could be true, but that isn’t all.

That Jang Ilso he met after three years wasn’t the Jang Ilso he knew at all.

In just three years, Jang Ilso had turned into someone who suited that throne so well. Even the great lord Desolate Gold Elder was overwhelmed by his momentum.

“... such a boring joke.”

Jang Ilso spoke in a languid voice.

“It hasn’t been long since the Black Dragon King lost his arm in the Plum Blossom Island, and now the Desolate Gold Elder shows up with his arm cut off... if this keeps up, I will have to bring in a prosthetic limb physician for the Four Sects Alliance.”

“....”

When there was no answer from Desolate Gold Elder, Jang Ilso shook his head. There was nothing more boring than talking to someone who doesn’t even understand a joke.

“Fine. Let us talk.”

At those words, Desolate Gold Elder’s blank eyes stared at Jang Ilso.

“You can try to make excuses or persuade me. I don’t know if any of that will save you from the arm you lost while fighting the Demonic Cult.”

A cruel gaze mixed with hint of laughter pierced Desolate Gold Elder. It was completely different from Desolate Gold Elder’s gaze which was amost close to a corpse.

However, Desolate Gold Elder didn’t particularly feel fear in those eyes. what he had experienced was too shocking to be afraid of those eyes.

“... High priest...”

Desolate Gold elder forced his lips which was not opening.

“That one... is no human.”

Jang Ilso’s two eyes sank gloomily.

“You have learned something great in return for giving up an arm.”

“... I am not making any excuses here, Alliance Lord.”

Desolate Gold Elder’s lips began to tremble slightly. As if even thinking about the high priest was a difficult and painful task for him.

“Nothing worked. Nothing... even the sword I put into the monster’s chest couldn’t hurt him.”

The moment those words came out, the eyes of Jang Ilso twitched.

Putting aside the personal feelings about Desolate Gold Elder, His Ghost King’s Incarnation which included 12 forms was a great sword technique which even Jang Ilso had to acknowledge.

But is there anyone in this world that Desolate Gold Elder couldn’t even hurt?

‘It isnt realistic at all.’

Jang Ilso leaned ahead slightly from his backrest.

“Give me more details.”

“In my life... this is the first time that I have encountered something like that. it isnt something that people can do anything about. Nothing works well... really, just nothing.”

“There is nothing in the world which people cannot do anything about.”

Jang Ilso calmly said.

“Kangho already drove those Demonic Cult people to ruin a hundred years ago. And now? What a low quality joke you say.”

“... A joke?”

Jang Ilso’s eyes flashed with annoyance at the question from Desolate Gold Elder.

He still thought it was nonsense, but he couldn’t ignore the issue, so all of this felt more uncomfortable. The reason was very simple. The one who was making that claim was none other than the Desolate Gold Elder.

If the person who had said this was the Black Dragon King or the Thousand Faced Scholar, he could have dismissed it as a scared guy speaking nonsense.

However, Desolate Gold Elder wasn’t like that. The Desolate Gold Elder that Jang Ilso knew wouldn’t lose his senses even if his head were about to be cut off.

“... I didn’t understand it. No... to be honest, I was scared.”

‘So...”

Jang Ilso opened his mouth in a serious tone.

“Are you saying that someone like you, so strong, couldn’t do anything against him? A high priest?”

Desolate Gold Elder hesitated instead of answering right away. It seemed like he, too, needed to organize his thoughts.

“... It is a bit different.”

“Different?”

“He was definitely strong. It was unbelievable too. He fought against me and a hundred elite warriors whom I led at the same time, all without him getting wounded.”

As if recalling the scene, Desolate Gold Elder shivered.

“Right... true. Now that I think about it, it surely is strange.”

“Stop with your mumbling and talk properly. Unless your head is also at a loss now.”

When Jang Ilso urged, Desolate Gold Elder hesitated for a moment and spoke in a slightly cracked voice.

“... I failed to show my skills.”

Jang Ilso’s eyes flashed with an odd color when he heard that.

“Be more specific now.”

“There wasn’t anything wrong with his body. I cannot deny that I was overwhelmed by his momentum, but that doesn’t explain it all. Even though I did my best, my sword didn’t feel like my sword at all. It was as if... yes, it was as if I was swinging my sword in deep water.”

Jang Ilso slowly licked his lips.

“Demonic Arts...”

The legends about the Demonic Cult were always absurd.

The most absurd thing of all is that they were never the majority, yet they always dominated the entire Central Plains.

No matter how fanatical they were, if they were beasts born from the same person, there wouldn’t be much difference in what they learned.

But, listening to the words of Desolate Gold Elder, it felt like the secret of their strangeness was somewhat resolved.

And Desolate Gold Elder added.

“... it was like we had encountered a wall. A wall that couldn’t be overcome by anything.”

Jang Ilso’s face hardened, and he was lost in thought.

Looking back, there were no Evil Sect people who gained fame in the battle against the Demonic Cult. In the previous war against the Demonic Cult, the Evil Sect was simply swept away helplessly.

It is natural for the Evil Sect to submit to the strong.

It is ridiculous, for sure.

Surrender only has meaning when survival is guaranteed in return. What is the point of submitting to the Demonic Cult, which doesn’t allow those who surrender to live?

At that time, the absolute leaders of the Evil Sect would certainly have fought the Demonic Cult. However, strangely, these people, who were clearly not behind the Justice faction, had no record against the Demonic Cult.

Then the interpretation becomes all too obvious.

“A matter of compatibility.”

“... Seems like it.”

Jang Ilso pressed his temples.

According to what Desolate Gold Elder said, the martial arts of the Demonic Cult might have been a kind of compatibility issue for the Evil Sect.

‘N-Not just for us.’

If that was the case, the Demonic Cult’s evil hand wouldn’t have been able to sweep away the entire martial arts sects. It should be seen as something that can swallow up the entire Central Plains.

‘But then, how?’

Is that realistically possible?

Who on earth could create something like that? If that is possible, then that ability must have already surpassed that of humans.

“... No, it can’t be.”

Jang Ilso’s fingers, which kept pressing down on his temples, gained strength.

‘No. This isn’t it.’

When a person like Jang Ilso encounters a situation, he tries to understand the cause, effect, and principles of the situation. This is because he feels uncomfortable with things that are not accompanied by understanding.

However, sometimes it is better to focus on the phenomenon itself rather than understanding. If you deny what you cannot interpret, you may eventually fall for your own tricks.

‘Interpret it as it is.’

Even if that doesn’t make sense, even if it is something that his head cannot understand, in this situation, acceptance will produce better results than denial.

“How many of them are there?”

“I couldn’t be sure of it properly.”

Jang Ilso frowned.

“But it is certain that they are not the majority. It seemed that there were no other high priests there besides the one I saw.”

“... so it means they are the vanguard or the group that broke away from the main force. No, maybe that is all that was left of the Demonic Cult.”

Desolate Gold Elder nodded as if agreeing.

“Then the solution is simple. We just have to kill that high priest by any means necessary.”

“Pushing ahead with numbers won’t work. His martial arts seem to be made for massacring people in numbers.”

“In other words, you ran after losing to him. You sure say good things after what you did. You don’t seem to even know shame, lord.”

“I understand that.”

Jang Ilso’s eyes dimmed for a moment.

But Desolate Gold Elder mumbled, staring blankly into space as if he couldn’t see the reactions of anyone.

“If you face that monster directly... you will understand that shame is nothing more than a luxury. You will surely know...”

“Tsk.”

Jang Ilso clicked his tongue as if he was annoyed. Then he leaned back in his chair.

‘Why Hangzhou of all places?’

The most important thing in war is the money. And the place where the Demonic Cult had set out was the wealthiest land in the Southern lands. If that place were to be burned down, it was obvious that the money collected by the Four Sects Alliance would be greatly affected.

No matter what, he had to resolve it as quickly as possible.

He closed his eyes quietly and calmly sorted out the situation.

‘The solution is...’

How much time had passed like this?

Jang Ilso’s eyes, when they opened, looked cold.

“... Immortal Path.”

“....”

“Immortals.”

Jang Ilso smiled crookedly as if he had finally found a solution.

“If those guys really have an incredible power that cannot be understood with common sense, then the only ones who can break them down are Immortals and Buddhists. I never thought I would believe in such nonsense as the spirits of destroying evil.”

He looked up at the ceiling as if he was in despair.

“But... rather than denying what happened, I think it would be better for me to turn into a foolish fanatic.”

Jang Ilso, who had been looking at the ceiling with his head up, finally slowly got up.

Desolate Gold Elder followed his movements with his eyes and asked.

“What are you planning to do?”

“I need a knife.”

Jang Ilso’s eyes turned cold.

“The situation isn’t that easy to handle. It doesn’t seem like an easy enemy to take on with two crippled men. If I step ahead and fail, it would be the worst. Then everything would fall apart. I absolutely cannot fail.”

“....”

“Then I need to obtain the right sword. A sword that can definitely cut off the monster’s head.”

Desolate Gold Elder is a man of extraordinary intelligence. Even without a detailed explanation, he clearly knew who Jang Ilso meant by ‘sword.’

“Do you think they will respond?”

“Hmm. Such nonsense, great lord. I am not asking them to respond. I am making them respond.”

“....”

A wicked laughter came from the mouth of Jang Ilso with sharp eyes.

“The world is so funny. Who would have thought that I would be reaching out to them? Hahahaha.”

His laughter shook the hall.

After laughing like that for a while, he walked ahead without hesitation, making loud clanging sounds.

Behind him, the Desolate Gold elder simply watched Jang Ilso’s moving body with both concern and anticipation.

Chapter 1029: But I Can Understand (4)

Hae Pyung, gazing at the setting sun, sighed unconsciously. Lately, he had been feeling suffocated.

‘I don’t even know what I’m doing.’

He couldn’t understand why he had to leave Mount Song and spend all his time here on the Yangtze River. No matter how many times he was told it was to monitor the Four Sects Alliance, it never quite made sense to him.

After all, all they did upon reaching the Yangtze River was observe the happenings on Plum Blossom Island from a distance.

In the end, it wasn’t Shaolin but the Heavenly Friends Alliance who fought the pirates. So hearing that he couldn’t leave the Yangtze River to deal with the Four Sects Alliance felt pointless.

Staring at the thick sunset, Hae Pyung sighed again and finally spoke.

“Sahyung.”

“... What is it?”

Hae Gong, standing beside him, answered without turning his head.

“Did Hae Bang sahyung arrive at Mount Song safely?”

There was a pause before a heavy voice replied.

“What? Do you want to return to Mount Song too?”

“It isn’t that... I just...”

Hae Pyung stopped mid-sentence and let out a deep sigh.

“I don’t know, sahyung. It’s true that Hae Bang sahyung was rude to the abbot, but is that really a mistake deserving admission into the repentance cave?”

“It was Hae Bang sahyung’s own decision to enter the repentance cave. It’s not a matter of right or wrong.”

“... Yes, that’s true.”

Hae Gong finally turned to look at Hae Pyung.

“Do you regret not following?”

Hae Pyung remained silent.

To be honest, he naturally wanted to follow Hae Bang back to Mount Song. The orders he had received from the abbot after arriving at the Yangtze River were incomprehensible to him.

He understood that all of it must have been for the sake of Shaolin. No one doubted that the abbot’s choices were based solely on concern for Shaolin.

But...

“I learned that Buddhism isn’t afraid to become stones for the growth of living beings.”

“....”

“Then we are....”

“Enough.”

Hae Gong cut him off before he could continue.

“Even if you have doubts, don’t voice them so easily. Once you say something like that, you can’t take it back.”

“....”

“If you have something to think about, think deeply. Only when you believe your thoughts aren’t completely wrong should you speak out. Otherwise, it’s just delusion.”

“I will keep that in mind, sahyung.”

A deep sigh escaped Hae Pyung’s lips.

He had thought about it occasionally. Was the reason he couldn’t follow Hae Bang simply because he lacked courage? Was it fear of going against the abbot’s orders and acting independently?

‘Maybe I’m not qualified for that.’

He was a Buddhist who had pledged to sacrifice his body for all living beings, yet he feared the abbot’s wrath and couldn’t do what he thought was right. Did he have the right to question the abbot about right and wrong?

Just as Hae Pyung was sinking into these thoughts, Hae Gong spoke.

“Someone is coming.”

“Yes?”

Hae Pyung raised his head and looked at the approaching figures.

‘Who...’

Would anyone else come here at this hour, with the sun setting?

Hae Pyung’s face hardened as he recalled his sahyung’s warning: ‘Do not trust new people.’

“Uh?”

But soon, a bewildered voice escaped his mouth.

Among the approaching group, the one at the front looked familiar.

“Sahyung. That guy... no, that man?”

“... I think you’re right.”

Hae Gong’s expression stiffened.

‘Why is that guy here?’

The one approaching was none other than Hyun Jong, the sect leader of Mount Hua.

Of course, there was no reason for Hyun Jong not to come here. However, considering the current strained ties between Shaolin and Mount Hua, it was clearly an awkward visit.

“How...”

“Do not act rashly.”

Hae Gong calmed Hae Pyung.

“Welcome him with manners.”

“Yes, sahyung.”

In those brief words, Hae Gong’s respect for Hyun Jong was evident. Although the relationship between Shaolin and Mount Hua had become strained, Hyun Jong was undoubtedly someone deserving respect and honor as a warrior, regardless of his affiliation.

When Hyun Jong arrived before them, Hae Gong greeted him politely as the sect leader.

“I greet you, sect leader.”

Hyun Jong bowed deeply, holding his hands—a natural courtesy.

“I am Hyun Jong of Mount Hua. Please understand my sudden, unannounced visit. I apologize for the inconvenience.”

“No, sect leader. It’s not an inconvenience.”

Hae Gong shook his head and asked casually.

“May I ask the reason for your visit?”

Hyun Jong took a deep breath and spoke.

“I have something to discuss. Please inform the abbot that Hyun Jong of Mount Hua... no, Hyun Jong of the Heavenly Friends Alliance wishes to meet him.”

“... The abbot?”

“Yes.”

Hae Gong looked flustered.

The abbot was currently not receiving visitors. However, the person standing before him was an outsider who could not simply be dismissed.

The leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance had come in person, so what use were the usual principles now?

After a moment’s thought, Hae Gong nodded.

“I’m sorry, but could you wait here for a moment? The abbot isn’t receiving visitors right now, so I should inform him first that the Alliance leader is here and ask for his opinion.”

“I can wait as long as you want.”

“Thank you. Then.”

Hae Gong nodded to Hae Pyung, quickly opened the door, and went inside.

Hyun Jong, watching them, looked at the hall with deep, sunken eyes.

“Sect leader.”

Hyun Young, standing behind him, spoke with concern.

“How about changing your mind now? At least make a prior appointment. The sight of us running and clinging to them won’t look good. If we continue like this, we’ll be laughed at.”

According to Kangho etiquette, they couldn’t say anything even if turned away. Yet Hyun Jong chose to come first, knowing this.

“I know.”

“Then...”

“But now is not the time to worry about that.”

Instead of looking at Hyun Young, Hyun Jong stared at the deserted hall.

—Just understand that.

He couldn’t care about saving face if he understood the situation. He couldn’t follow etiquette if he understood it. Being a laughingstock wasn’t worth a penny compared to the sacrifices of those losing their lives at that moment.

Ad...

“The situation in the Southern Lands is their situation...”

Hyun Jong bit his lip slightly.

“I’m worried about how long that kid will hold back.”

“... You mean Chung Myung?”

“Yes.”

“No matter how special Chung Myung is, he cannot disobey the orders of the sect leader. If he does, he should be severely punished.”

Although Hyun Young always defended Chung Myung, he now showed a firm stance. This could possibly shake the foundation of the sect and the Alliance.

However, Hyun Jong’s thoughts differed.

“It isn’t the Taoist’s job to create an unavoidable situation just to test whether someone will obey.”

“... Those are true words, but...”

“The best course is to find a better way for both of us. I don’t want to be a leader who oppresses my disciples. I want to be a leader they can trust.”

Hyun Young nodded.

But even if their hearts were aligned, it was questionable how Shaolin would respond...

At that moment, Hae Gong returned swiftly and politely addressed Hyun Jong.

“The abbot will see you. I will escort you.”

“Thank you.”

Hyun Jong stepped into the room with a nervous look.

His hand twitched slightly on his knee, as if uncomfortable.

Isn’t it said that the true nature of a relationship is revealed when you sit face to face?

In that sense, it wouldn’t be an exaggeration to say that the relationship between the person sitting before him now and Hyun Jong was the worst. Even if Southern Edge’s leader or Jang Ilso were sitting there, he wouldn’t feel so uncomfortable.

However, this opponent didn’t seem particularly uneasy with Hyun Jong’s presence; his expression was relaxed.

“Yes...”

The person sitting before Hyun Jong—the abbot—slowly spoke while lifting the teacup in front of him.

“Why did the Heavenly Friends Alliance leader come to see me?”

Hot steam rose from the teacup. Watching it dissipate, Hyun Jong suppressed a sigh that almost escaped.

Actually, the relationship between the abbot and him hadn’t always been like this. When he first met the abbot at the Heavenly Martial Arts Tournament, he didn’t harbor such ill feelings.

No, maybe he even admired the man a little. He was the abbot of the Great Mountain’s Shaolin in Kangho, a ruler of the world in a position Hyun Jong wouldn’t dare to take.

But now...

“Hmm.”

The abbot smiled brightly, as if he guessed Hyun Jong’s feelings.

“It’s been several years since we first met.”

“... Yes, abbot.”

“I think I poured you tea right here back then.”

“Yes. I still remember what you said.”

“Hmm. What I said back then...”

Hyun Jong quietly looked at the abbot and said,

“You told me you were flustered by the tea I poured and said this: Tea is just tea. Whether it’s the tea the emperor pours or the tea the commoner pours, it’s just tea.”

The abbot nodded.

“Those words hit me hard at the time. Even if it’s the same words, their weight varies depending on who speaks them. When the abbot of Shaolin said those words, my heavy shoulders felt a little lighter.”

The abbot’s eyes twitched.

Hyun Jong might just be reminiscing, but to the abbot’s ears, those words sounded like a question: ‘Are you the same as before or not?’

How is it? Is the abbot of then the same as the abbot of now?

The abbot didn’t want to answer that question directly. He simply said,

“The tree is just there, but the wind doesn’t leave it alone.”

Hyun Jong nodded in agreement.

“The world is funny, alliance leader.”

The abbot smiled.

“Who would have guessed back then that we’d become awkward even sharing a cup of tea?”

“It is truly embarrassing.”

After a brief, awkward silence, the abbot looked silently at his teacup. The steaming tea cooled slowly. People are the same—time changes everything, even if they do nothing.

“Yes. Why did you come to me? It must not have been easy.”

Hyun Jong let out a soft sigh at the abbot’s question.
